  2027 |   const baseAbbrevFromName = (name) => {
  2028 |     const cleanName = String(name || "").trim();
  2029 |     const upper = cleanName.toUpperCase();
  2030 |     const words = upper.replace(/[^A-Z0-9 ]/g, " ").split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  2031 | 
  2032 |     if (!words.length) return "";
  2033 | 
  2034 |     const compact = words.join("");
  2035 | 
  2036 |     if (/^DARK/.test(compact)) return "DARK";
  2037 |     if (/^LIGHT/.test(compact)) return "LIGT";
  2038 | 
  2039 |     if (words[0] === "MAN" || words[0] === "MANCHESTER") {
  2040 |       const second = words[1] || "";
  2041 |       if (second.startsWith("C")) return "MCI";
  2042 |       if (second.startsWith("U")) return "MUN";
  2043 |     }
  2044 | 
  2045 |     if (compact.startsWith("MADRID")) return "MAD";
  2046 |     if (compact.startsWith("BARCELONA")) return "BAR";
  2047 |     if (compact.startsWith("AJAX")) return "AJA";
  2048 | 
  2049 |     if (words.length >= 2) {
  2050 |       const initials = words.map((word) => word[0]).join("");
  2051 |       if (initials.length >= 3) return initials.slice(0, 4);
  2052 |     }
  2053 | 
  2054 |     return compact.slice(0, compact.length >= 4 ? 4 : 3);
  2055 |   };
  2056 | 
  2057 |   const makeUniqueTeamAbbrev = (teamId, nextName, teamsList = sourceTeams) => {
  2058 |     const preferred = normalizeAbbrev(baseAbbrevFromName(nextName));
  2059 |     if (!preferred) return "";
  2060 | 
  2061 |     const taken = new Set(
  2062 |       (teamsList || [])
  2063 |         .filter((team) => team.id !== teamId)
  2064 |         .map((team) => normalizeAbbrev(team.abbrev || baseAbbrevFromName(team.label)))
  2065 |         .filter(Boolean)
  2066 |     );
  2067 | 
  2068 |     if (!taken.has(preferred)) return preferred;
  2069 | 
  2070 |     const compact = String(nextName || "").toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g, "");
  2071 |     const candidates = [
  2072 |       compact.slice(0, 4),
  2073 |       compact.slice(0, 3),
  2074 |       ...compact.split("").map((_, index) => `${preferred.slice(0, 3)}${compact[index] || ""}`),
  2075 |     ]

  2077 |       .filter((value) => value.length >= 3 && value.length <= 4);
  2078 | 
  2079 |     return candidates.find((candidate) => candidate && !taken.has(candidate)) || preferred;
  2080 |   };

    68 |   raw = raw.replace(/\s+/g, "").replace(/[()-]/g, "");
    69 | 
    70 |   if (raw.startsWith("whatsapp:")) raw = raw.slice(9);
    71 | 
    72 |   if (raw.startsWith("+")) {
    73 |     const digits = `+${raw.slice(1).replace(/\D/g, "")}`;
    74 |     return /^\+\d{9,15}$/.test(digits) ? digits : "";
    75 |   }
    76 | 
    77 |   const digitsOnly = raw.replace(/\D/g, "");
    78 |   if (!digitsOnly) return "";
    79 | 
    80 |   if (digitsOnly.startsWith("27") && digitsOnly.length === 11) {
    81 |     return `+${digitsOnly}`;
    82 |   }
    83 | 
    84 |   if (digitsOnly.startsWith("0") && digitsOnly.length === 10) {
    85 |     return `+27${digitsOnly.slice(1)}`;
    86 |   }
    87 | 
    88 |   if (digitsOnly.length >= 9 && digitsOnly.length <= 15) {
    89 |     return `+${digitsOnly}`;
    90 |   }
    91 | 
    92 |   return "";
    93 | }

   334 | function getPrimarySignupScopeMonth() {
   335 |   const now = new Date();
   336 |   const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
   337 |   const shouldShiftToNextMonth = today.getDate() >= 25;
   338 |   const targetYear = shouldShiftToNextMonth
   339 |     ? now.getMonth() === 11
   340 |       ? now.getFullYear() + 1
   341 |       : now.getFullYear()
   342 |     : now.getFullYear();
   343 |   const targetMonth = shouldShiftToNextMonth
   344 |     ? (now.getMonth() + 1) % 12
   345 |     : now.getMonth();
   346 | 
   347 |   return new Date(targetYear, targetMonth, 1).toLocaleDateString("en-ZA", {
   348 |     year: "numeric",
   349 |     month: "2-digit",
   350 |   });

   353 | function getMonthWednesdays({ visibleOnly = true, settings = DEFAULT_MATCH_SIGNUP_SETTINGS } = {}) {
   354 |   const mergedSettings = mergeMatchSignupSettings(settings);
   355 |   const now = new Date();
   356 |   const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());
   357 |   const shouldShiftToNextMonth = today.getDate() >= 25;
   358 | 
   359 |   const monthTargets = [];
   360 | 
   361 |   monthTargets.push({
   362 |     year: now.getFullYear(),
   363 |     month: now.getMonth(),
   364 |     includeOnlyFuture: visibleOnly,
   365 |   });
   366 | 
   367 |   if (shouldShiftToNextMonth) {
   368 |     monthTargets.push({
   369 |       year: now.getMonth() === 11 ? now.getFullYear() + 1 : now.getFullYear(),
   370 |       month: (now.getMonth() + 1) % 12,
   371 |       includeOnlyFuture: false,
   372 |     });
   373 |   }
   374 | 
   375 |   const byId = new Map();
   376 | 
   377 |   monthTargets.forEach(({ year, month, includeOnlyFuture }) => {
   378 |     const d = new Date(year, month, 1);
   379 | 
   380 |     while (d.getMonth() === month) {
   381 |       const candidate = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate());
   382 |       const isTargetWeekday = candidate.getDay() === Number(mergedSettings.weeklyDay);
   383 |       const shouldInclude = includeOnlyFuture ? candidate >= today : true;
   384 | 
   385 |       if (isTargetWeekday && shouldInclude) {
   386 |         const matchDay = buildMatchDayFromDate(candidate, {
   387 |           type: "weekly",
   388 |           title: getWeekdayName(mergedSettings.weeklyDay),
   389 |           maxPlayers: MAX_PLAYERS,
   390 |           costPerGame: mergedSettings.weeklyPrice,
   391 |         });
   392 |         byId.set(matchDay.id, matchDay);
   393 |       }
   394 | 
   395 |       d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
   396 |     }
   397 |   });
   398 | 
   399 |   const challenge = mergedSettings.challenge || {};
   400 |   if (challenge.enabled && challenge.date) {
   401 |     const challengeDate = new Date(`${challenge.date}T12:00:00`);
   402 |     if (!Number.isNaN(challengeDate.getTime())) {
   403 |       const shouldIncludeChallenge = visibleOnly
   404 |         ? challengeDate >= today
   405 |         : true;
   406 | 
   407 |       if (shouldIncludeChallenge) {
   408 |         const challengeMatchDay = buildMatchDayFromDate(challengeDate, {
   409 |           id: challenge.date,
   410 |           isChallenge: true,
   411 |           title: challenge.title || "Challenge",
   412 |           maxPlayers: challenge.maxPlayers,
   413 |           costPerGame: challenge.price,
   414 |         });
   415 |         byId.set(challengeMatchDay.id, challengeMatchDay);
   416 |       }
   417 |     }
   418 |   }
   419 | 
   420 |   return Array.from(byId.values()).sort((a, b) => a.date - b.date);
   421 | }

   423 | function getCalendarMonthData(weeks = []) {
   424 |   const firstWeekDate =
   425 |     Array.isArray(weeks) && weeks.length > 0 ? weeks[0].date : null;
   426 | 
   427 |   const baseDate = firstWeekDate instanceof Date ? firstWeekDate : new Date();
   428 |   const year = baseDate.getFullYear();
   429 |   const month = baseDate.getMonth();
   430 | 
   431 |   const firstDay = new Date(year, month, 1);
   432 |   const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate();
   433 |   const startWeekday = firstDay.getDay();
   434 | 
   435 |   const cells = [];
   436 | 
   437 |   for (let i = 0; i < startWeekday; i += 1) cells.push(null);
   438 | 
   439 |   for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day += 1) {
   440 |     const date = new Date(year, month, day);
   441 |     const id = `${year}-${String(month + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(
   442 |       day
   443 |     ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   444 | 
   445 |     cells.push({
   446 |       id,
   447 |       day,
   448 |       weekday: date.getDay(),
   449 |       date,
   450 |     });
   451 |   }
   452 | 
   453 |   return {
   454 |     monthLabel: firstDay.toLocaleDateString("en-ZA", {
   455 |       month: "long",
   456 |       year: "numeric",
   457 |     }),
   458 |     cells,
   459 |   };
   460 | }

   462 | function getCalendarMonthsData(weeks = []) {
   463 |   const safeWeeks = Array.isArray(weeks) ? weeks : [];
   464 |   const monthKeys = [];
   465 | 
   466 |   safeWeeks.forEach((week) => {
   467 |     if (!(week?.date instanceof Date)) return;
   468 |     const key = `${week.date.getFullYear()}-${String(week.date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   469 |     if (!monthKeys.includes(key)) monthKeys.push(key);
   470 |   });
   471 | 
   472 |   if (!monthKeys.length) return [getCalendarMonthData([])];
   473 | 
   474 |   return monthKeys.map((key) => {
   475 |     const [yearRaw, monthRaw] = key.split("-");
   476 |     const year = Number(yearRaw);
   477 |     const month = Number(monthRaw) - 1;
   478 | 
   479 |     return getCalendarMonthData([
   480 |       {
   481 |         date: new Date(year, month, 1),
   482 |       },
   483 |     ]);
   484 |   });
   485 | }

   488 |   const cap = Number(maxPlayers || MAX_PLAYERS);

   500 |   }
   501 |   return { key: "low", label: "needs players", shortLabel: "not filled" };

    17 | 
    18 | function isChallengerChatActive(fixture = {}) {
    19 |   const status = String(fixture?.status || "").toLowerCase();
    20 | 
    21 |   if (
    22 |     status.includes("cancel") ||
    23 |     status.includes("rejected") ||
    24 |     status.includes("declined")
    25 |   ) {
    26 |     return false;
    27 |   }
    28 | 
    29 |   const dateText = String(fixture?.proposedDate || "").trim();
    30 |   if (!dateText) return true;
    31 | 
    32 |   const kickoffText = String(fixture?.proposedKickoff || "23:59").trim();
    33 |   const timeText = /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(kickoffText) ? kickoffText : "23:59";
    34 |   const fixtureTime = new Date(`${dateText}T${timeText}:00`);
    35 | 
    36 |   if (Number.isNaN(fixtureTime.getTime())) return true;
    37 | 
    38 |   return fixtureTime.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 >= Date.now();
    39 | }

   258 | function buildPlayerResolver(players = []) {
   259 |   const byAny = new Map();

   318 |       };

  2715 |     async function requestScreenWakeLock() {
  2716 |       try {
  2717 |         if (!("wakeLock" in navigator)) return;
  2718 |         if (wakeLockRef.current) return;
  2719 | 
  2720 |         const lock = await navigator.wakeLock.request("screen");
  2721 |         if (!mounted) {
  2722 |           lock.release?.().catch(() => {});
  2723 |           return;
  2724 |         }
  2725 | 
  2726 |         wakeLockRef.current = lock;
  2727 | 
  2728 |         lock.addEventListener?.("release", () => {
  2729 |           if (mounted) wakeLockRef.current = null;
  2730 |         });
  2731 |       } catch (err) {
  2732 |         // Some browsers/devices do not support wake lock. Silent fallback is fine.
  2733 |         console.warn("Screen wake lock unavailable:", err);
  2734 |       }
  2735 |     }

  1065 | async function appendEventToFirestore(
  1066 |   event,
  1067 |   summaryInfo,
  1068 |   secondsLeft,
  1069 |   matchSeconds
  1070 | ) {
  1071 |   try {
  1072 |     const ref = getMatchDoc(db, MATCH_DOC_ID);
  1073 | 
  1074 |     const common = {
  1075 |       ...summaryInfo,
  1076 |       matchMode: summaryInfo.matchMode || "5_V_5",
  1077 |       gameFormat: summaryInfo.gameFormat || "5_V_5",
  1078 |       playersPerSide: summaryInfo.playersPerSide || 5,
  1079 |       matchSeconds: matchSeconds ?? 0,
  1080 |       secondsLeft:
  1081 |         typeof secondsLeft === "number" ? Math.max(secondsLeft, 0) : null,
  1082 |       isFinished: false,
  1083 |       lastUpdated: serverTimestamp(),
  1084 |     };
  1085 | 
  1086 |     try {
  1087 |       await updateDoc(ref, {
  1088 |         events: arrayUnion(event),
  1089 |         ...common,
  1090 |       });
  1091 |     } catch (_) {
  1092 |       await setDoc(
  1093 |         ref,
  1094 |         {
  1095 |           events: [event],
  1096 |           createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  1097 |           ...common,
  1098 |         },
  1099 |         { merge: true }
  1100 |       );
  1101 |     }
  1102 |   } catch (err) {
  1103 |     console.error("⚠️ Failed to mirror event to Firestore:", err);
  1104 |   }
  1105 | }

   213 |   const b = parseInt(clean.slice(4, 6), 16);
   214 |   return `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, ${alpha})`;

  1873 | function secondsLeftFromExpectedEnd(expectedEndAtISO) {
  1874 |   const endMs = new Date(expectedEndAtISO || "").getTime();
  1875 |   if (!Number.isFinite(endMs)) return null;
  1876 |   return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((endMs - Date.now()) / 1000));
  1877 | }

  1879 | function addSecondsToISO(startISO, seconds) {

  1881 |   const safeStart = Number.isFinite(startMs) ? startMs : Date.now();
  1882 |   return new Date(safeStart + Math.max(0, Number(seconds || 0)) * 1000).toISOString();
  1883 | }

  2019 | function parseClubWeeklyWindow(text) {

  2022 | 
  2023 |   const dayMap = {
  2024 |     sunday: 0, sundays: 0,
  2025 |     monday: 1, mondays: 1,
  2026 |     tuesday: 2, tuesdays: 2,
  2027 |     wednesday: 3, wednesdays: 3,
  2028 |     thursday: 4, thursdays: 4,
  2029 |     friday: 5, fridays: 5,
  2030 |     saturday: 6, saturdays: 6,
  2031 |   };

  2033 |   const dayKey = Object.keys(dayMap).find((key) => raw.includes(key));
  2034 |   const timeMatch = raw.match(/(\d{1,2})[:h](\d{2})(?:\s*[–-]\s*(\d{1,2})[:h](\d{2}))?/);
  2035 | 
  2036 |   if (!dayKey || !timeMatch) return null;
  2037 | 
  2038 |   const [, sh, sm, rawEh, rawEm] = timeMatch;
  2039 |   const eh = rawEh || String(Number(sh) + 2);
  2040 |   const em = rawEm || sm;
  2041 |   return {
  2042 |     day: dayMap[dayKey],
  2043 |     startMinutes: Number(sh) * 60 + Number(sm),
  2044 |     endMinutes: Number(eh) * 60 + Number(em),
  2045 |   };
  2046 | }

  2048 | function isInsideClubWeeklyWindow(weeklyPlayTime, now = new Date()) {
  2049 |   const windowInfo = parseClubWeeklyWindow(weeklyPlayTime);
  2050 |   if (!windowInfo) return false;
  2051 | 
  2052 |   const currentDay = now.getDay();
  2053 |   const currentMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes();
  2054 | 
  2055 |   // 30-minute grace before and after the official window.
  2056 |   return (
  2057 |     currentDay === windowInfo.day &&
  2058 |     currentMinutes >= windowInfo.startMinutes - 30 &&
  2059 |     currentMinutes <= windowInfo.endMinutes + 30
  2060 |   );
  2061 | }
